Arm A0: build M1 yourself in this one session, in plan order: no subagents, no workflows, no build skill. Start with `dt build start M1` (it creates build/M1, where you commit). Then for each task in tasks/plan.md order (re-read the plan after any Replan):
1. `dt context <task>` and read it in full; `git update-ref refs/worktree/checks-base HEAD`.
2. A split task: write its intent tests (its `Writes:` under tests/intent/) first, without the code; `scripts/check.sh collect` must pass (red for the right reason); commit them with the message `<task>: tests` and the trailer line `Dt-Landed: <task> tester`; `git update-ref refs/worktree/checks-base HEAD`.
3. Write the rest of its `Writes:` and nothing else; `scripts/check.sh gate --pkg <its Package>` must pass; `dt tick <task>`; commit everything with the message `<task>: <title>` and the trailer line `Dt-Landed: <task> <implementer for a split task, else solo>`.
A re-freeze interface task names frozen contract folders in its `Writes:`: list them in `.claude/.task_writes`, one per line, before its gate, and delete the file after. One git command per Bash call. When every task is ticked, report and ask me before merging build/M1.

66	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
67	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
68	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
69	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability).
```python
def vol_series(path, *, window=20, tickers=None) -> VolSeries:
    prices = load_prices(path, tickers)
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, window)
    return annualize(vol)
```

```python
def risk_report(path, out_path, *, window=20, tickers=None) -> Report:
    vol = vol_series(path, window=window, tickers=tickers)
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(vol.returns)
    return write_report(summary, drawdowns, out_path)
```

**Kind:** type · **Sample:** `tests/contracts/mdata.RawRow/sample.json` · **Cases:** eight rows of two tickers, one with a blank close

- **Used by:** PL-01

`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Prices: dates: tuple[str, ...]; tickers: tuple[str, ...]; closes: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]`
- **Invariants:** dates ascending; tickers sorted; every ticker has one positive close per date

### mdata.load_prices: Prices from a CSV
`def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices`
- **Invariants:** DATA-01–DATA-04 hold for the result
- **Errors:** `KeyError` for a requested ticker with no usable row; `GapError` for a gap over 5 dates; `ValueError` for a malformed file
- **Boundary:** risk · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/mdata.load_prices/ · **Cases:** two tickers over six dates
- **Used by:** risk; PL-01, PL-02

### risk.vol_series: Annualized rolling volatility of a CSV
`def vol_series(path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> VolSeries`
- **Invariants:** RISK-01–RISK-03 hold; `annualized` is True
- **Errors:** as `mdata.load_prices`; `ValueError` if `window` < 2
- **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/risk.vol_series/ · **Cases:** two tickers, window 2
- **Used by:** PL-02, PL-03

3	**Package:** mdata · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Architecture:** [architecture.md](architecture.md)

5	## Types
6	
7	### mdata.RawRow: One CSV row
8	**Kind:** type · **Sample:** `tests/contracts/mdata.RawRow/sample.json` · **Cases:** eight rows of two tickers, one with a blank close
9	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RawRow: date: str; ticker: str; close: float | None`
10	- **Invariants:** `date` is ISO `yyyy-mm-dd`; `close` is None for a blank cell
11	- **Used by:** PL-01
12	
13	### mdata.Prices: Aligned daily closes
14	**Kind:** type · **Sample:** `tests/contracts/mdata.Prices/sample.json` · **Cases:** two tickers over six aligned dates
15	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Prices: dates: tuple[str, ...]; tickers: tuple[str, ...]; closes: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]`
16	- **Invariants:** dates ascending; tickers sorted; every ticker has one positive close per date
17	- **Used by:** PL-01, PL-02
18	
19	### mdata.GapError: Gap too long to fill
20	**Kind:** type
21	`class GapError(ValueError)`
22	- **Used by:** PL-01
23	
24	## Functions
25	
26	### mdata.load_prices: Prices from a CSV
27	`def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices`
28	- **Invariants:** DATA-01–DATA-04 hold for the result
29	- **Errors:** `KeyError` for a requested ticker with no usable row; `GapError` for a gap over 5 dates; `ValueError` for a malformed file
30	- **Boundary:** risk · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/mdata.load_prices/ · **Cases:** two tickers over six dates
31	- **Used by:** risk; PL-01, PL-02
32	
33	### mdata.read_csv: Read the rows
34	`def read_csv(path: str | Path) -> list[RawRow]`
35	- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a missing column or a malformed date
36	- **Used by:** PL-01
37	
38	### mdata.validate_rows: Usable rows, sorted
39	`def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]`
40	- **Invariants:** positive closes only; one row per (date, ticker), the later one in file order; sorted by (date, ticker)
41	- **Errors:** `KeyError` for a requested ticker with no usable row
42	- **Used by:** PL-01
43	
44	### mdata.align_calendar: One calendar
45	`def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices`
46	- **Invariants:** starts on the first date every ticker has a close; gaps take the previous close
47	- **Errors:** `GapError` naming the ticker when more than `max_gap` dates in a row are missing
48	- **Used by:** PL-01

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# One entry point for every quality check. Hooks call it; so do CI and people.
3	# Written by dev-team2 build-setup. Change it through build-setup (Revise), not by hand:
4	# CONSTRAINTS.md names the rule each step enforces.
5	#
6	#   scripts/check.sh fast FILE...        per edit, instant               (PostToolUse: tester, implementer)
7	#   scripts/check.sh collect             tester's finish gate            (Stop: tester)
8	#   scripts/check.sh gate [--pkg ID]     implementer's finish gate       (Stop: implementer and solo; dt land)
9	#   scripts/check.sh full [--pkg ID]     checkpoints, CI                 (reviewer)
10	#   scripts/check.sh full --exit         the milestone exit: also no interface stub left
11	#   scripts/check.sh protected           print the protected-path globs  (path_guard.sh deny --protected)
12	#
13	# The base is refs/worktree/checks-base, one per worktree (dt claim sets it in a builder's worktree,
14	# dt land in the main checkout), else HEAD.
15	#
16	# tests/contracts/** in the protected list means the boundary contracts frozen at the base (CR-09);
17	# a contract folder new since the base belongs to the interface task freezing it, which may write it,
18	# and so does a frozen folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (a re-freeze interface task).
19	#
20	# collect runs the intent tests changed since the base and fails unless they are red for the right
21	# reason (scripts/red_check.py): AssertionError or an interface stub's NotImplementedError, never
22	# an ImportError or AttributeError.
23	#
24	# --pkg ID runs the tests of package ID and of every package that depends on it (multi-package:
25	# deptry too); gate and full also run tests/contracts, whole, when it exists. Lint, types, size
26	# and boundaries always cover the whole repo; they take seconds.
27	# FILE paths are absolute or relative to the project root.
28	# Prints every failure (it does not stop at the first) and exits 1 if any step failed.
29	set -u

41	# A package (or, in a single package, a component) and every package that depends on it,
42	# from the architecture's dependency rules.

46	    risk) echo "risk" ;;
47	    *) return 1 ;;
48	  esac
49	}

78	# A step that is not a Python tool (a script, git, another toolchain).
79	step() {
80	  echo "-> $*" >&2
81	  "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }

84	# A tool from the project's locked environment, never whatever is first on PATH.
85	run() {

90	# A measured check that fails only at the milestone exit: it prints its findings, and the reviewer
91	# records them, but a mid-milestone run passes.
92	report() {
93	  echo "-> $* (reported; fails only with --exit)" >&2
94	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || echo "(reported, not failing: $*)" >&2
95	}

97	# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
98	# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
99	run_tests() {
100	  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
101	  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
102	  local status=$?
103	  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
104	  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }
105	}

107	# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
108	# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
109	BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
110	base_commit() {
111	  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
112	}
113	
114	# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
115	# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
116	# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
117	# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
118	# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
119	CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
120	claimed_contract() {
121	  local line
122	  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
123	  while IFS= read -r line; do
124	    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
125	    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
126	    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
127	  done <.claude/.task_writes
128	  return 1
129	}
130	frozen_contracts() {
131	  local base meta path
132	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
133	  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
134	    case "$meta" in
135	      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
136	      *) echo "$path" ;;
137	    esac
138	  done
139	}
140	
141	# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
142	protected_globs() {
143	  local g
144	  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
145	    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
146	  done
147	}
148	
149	# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
150	unchanged_since_base() {
151	  local label="$1" base new
152	  shift
153	  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
154	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
155	    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
156	    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
157	  fi
158	  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
159	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
160	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
161	    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
162	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
163	  fi
164	}
165	
166	# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
167	only_intent_changed() {
168	  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
169	  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
170	  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
171	}
172	
173	# The intent test files the tester changed since the base, committed or not.
174	changed_intent_tests() {
175	  local base
176	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
177	  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
178	    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
179	    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
180	}
181	
182	# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
183	test_dirs() {
184	  local scope="" d p
185	  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
186	  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
187	    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
188	      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
189	    else
190	      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
191	    fi
192	  done
193	  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
194	}
195	
196	# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
197	dependencies() {
198	  local p scope
199	  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
200	    run deptry . --no-ansi
201	    return
202	  fi
203	  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
204	    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
205	  else
206	    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
207	  fi
208	  for p in $scope; do
209	    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
210	    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
211	  done
212	}
213	
214	static_checks() {
215	  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
216	  run ruff format --check .
217	  run "$TYPE_GATE"
218	  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
219	  dependencies
220	  run lint-imports --no-logo
221	}
222	
223	case "$mode" in
224	  fast)
225	    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
226	    files=()
227	    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
228	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
229	    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
230	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
231	    run typos "${files[@]}"
232	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
233	    ;;
234	  collect)
235	    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
236	      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
237	      exit 1
238	    fi
239	    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
240	    run ruff format --check tests/intent
241	    run typos tests/intent
242	    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
243	    changed=()
244	    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
245	    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
246	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
247	    only_intent_changed
248	    ;;
249	  gate)
250	    static_checks
251	    other_parts
252	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
253	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
254	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
255	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
256	    protected=()
257	    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
258	    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
259	    ;;
260	  full)
261	    static_checks
262	    other_parts
263	    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
264	    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
265	    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
266	    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
267	    run typos
268	    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
269	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
270	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
271	    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
272	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
273	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
274	    ;;
275	  protected)
276	    protected_globs
277	    ;;
278	  *)
279	    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
280	    exit 64
281	    ;;
282	esac
283	[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
284	exit $rc

# Minilab — Spec

**Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Brief:** [brief.md](brief.md)

## Features

### Prices (`DATA`)
**Serves:** Goal 1

**Requirements**
- **DATA-01** A `date,ticker,close` CSV is read row by row.
  - Given a CSV, when it is read, then each row comes back in file order, and a blank close is missing, not zero.
- **DATA-02** Unusable rows are dropped and duplicates resolved.
  - Given rows with blank, zero or negative closes and a repeated (date, ticker), when validated, then those rows are gone, the later of the duplicates wins, and rows are sorted by date, then ticker.
  - Given a requested ticker with no usable row, when validated, then a `KeyError` names it.
- **DATA-03** Every ticker sits on one calendar.
  - Given tickers that start on different dates and miss some dates, when aligned, then the calendar is the dates any ticker trades from the first date on which every ticker has a close, and a missing date takes that ticker's previous close.
- **DATA-04** A long gap is an error, not a fill.
  - Given a ticker missing more than 5 calendar dates in a row, when aligned, then a `GapError` names the ticker.

### Risk (`RISK`)
**Serves:** Goal 2

**Requirements**
- **RISK-01** Daily returns are log returns.
  - Given closes, when returns are computed, then r_t = ln(close_t / close_t−1), dated t.
- **RISK-02** Rolling volatility uses only earlier returns.
  - Given a window of w, when the volatility for date t is computed, then it is the sample standard deviation (n − 1) of the w returns before t, never t's own return, and there is no value until w earlier returns exist.
- **RISK-03** Volatility is annualized over trading days.
  - Given a daily volatility σ, when annualized, then the value is σ·√252.
- **RISK-04** Each ticker gets a whole-period summary.
  - Given returns, when summarized, then the annualized return is 252 × the mean, the annualized volatility is √252 × the sample standard deviation, the Sharpe ratio is their quotient (0 when the volatility is 0; no risk-free rate), and the last rolling volatility is reported.
- **RISK-05** Maximum drawdown comes with its dates.
  - Given closes, when the drawdown is computed, then wealth starts at 1 on the first date, and the result is the deepest fall below the running peak (trough / peak − 1, at most 0) with the date of that peak and the date of the trough; the first if tied. With no fall, both dates are the first date.

### Report (`REP`)
**Serves:** Goal 3

**Requirements**
- **REP-01** The risk report is a JSON file.
  - Given a prices CSV, when the report runs, then `{"tickers": [...]}` is written with one row per ticker, sorted, holding the RISK-04 fields and RISK-05's drawdown (as `max_drawdown`) and both dates.

### Quality
- **QR-01** A run is deterministic.
  - Given the same CSV and window, when the report runs twice, then the files are identical.

## Changelog
| Date | Change | Source | IDs |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-17 | Initial spec | brief.md | DATA-01–DATA-04, RISK-01–RISK-05, REP-01, QR-01 |

# risk — Tests

**Expected:** each value scaled, gaps kept · **Tolerance:** 1e-12 absolute

**Expected:** X −20% from the second date to the fourth; Y 0 at the first date; Z peaks at the first date · **Tolerance:** 1e-12 absolute on depth, dates exact

          {status,next-id,section,context,ready,plan,layout,outline,references,probes,lint,roadmap,guard,prebuild-clear,archive,finding,tick,base,claim,land,escalate,build} ...

dt: dev-team's doc tool. One parser for every planning doc; see
scripts/README.md for the commands.

positional arguments:
  {status,next-id,section,context,ready,plan,layout,outline,references,probes,lint,roadmap,guard,prebuild-clear,archive,finding,tick,base,claim,land,escalate,build}
    status              What a skill needs before it starts: mode, upstream
                        gates, gaps and next IDs. `--summary` for
                        SessionStart.
    next-id             Print the next free ID for a prefix (PL, TC, AD, CR,
                        S, M, M2-T, M2-R, or a spec prefix), retired IDs
                        counted.
    section             Print the sections that define the given IDs,
                        searching every doc (the archive last).
    context             Print a task's whole builder context packet: its Read:
                        sections and files, the package layout, the frozen
    ready               Every task of the active plan that can start now; with
                        --halves or --reserve, the build's task halves.
    plan                The plan as one snapshot for workflow-analyzer's plan
                        map (`plan-map/2`, interface-first-design.md §8.3):
                        goals,
    layout              Print a package's real source tree with module
                        docstrings, and flag entry functions that drift from
                        their step tables.
    outline             Print a file's outline: defs and classes with
                        signatures and docstring first lines (`.py`, via ast;
                        never
    references          Reference files against docs/references.md: list each
                        entry's state, or `--stamp` its Hash (mechanical).
    probes              Probe folders against docs/probes.md: list each
                        probe's state, `--stamp` its Hash (mechanical), or
                        `--scan` its
    lint                Check the planning docs: IDs, references, size
                        budgets, task rules, fences and headers.
    roadmap             Rewrite the roadmap's derived Status* columns:
                        milestones Idea → Specced → Designed → Planned →
                        Building →
    guard               PreToolUse guard: keep the agent out of secrets and a
                        probe's raw output (Read included), deny writes to the
    prebuild-clear      Replace a pipeline's pre-build entry sketch with a `dt
                        layout` pointer (and the planned layout once all are
                        built).
    archive             Archive a finished milestone: plan + todo to
                        tasks/archive/, a between-milestones stub plan, the
                        roadmap outcome.
    finding             Record a review finding under a checkpoint in
                        tasks/plan.md; --from records a reviewer's whole file.
    tick                Tick (or untick) a task's checkbox in tasks/todo.md.
    base                Record the milestone's base commit: set the active
                        plan header's `**Base:**` to HEAD's short sha.
    claim               Claim a reserved task half inside the agent's worktree
                        and write its packet to .claude/packet.md.
    land                Land a claimed task half from its worktree onto the
                        build branch; prints one JSON object.
    escalate            Record why this worktree's agent can't finish its half
                        (TEST-WRONG, CONTRACT-CHANGE, NEEDS-DEP).
    build               Run a milestone build: start, status, resolve,
                        release; verdict, escalated, mark-finished for hooks.

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --root ROOT           project root (default: current directory)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# PreToolUse hook: restrict which files an agent may touch.
#   path_guard.sh deny  GLOB...        block files matching any GLOB
#   path_guard.sh deny  --protected    block the protected list (scripts/check.sh protected)
#   path_guard.sh allow GLOB...        block files NOT matching any GLOB
#   path_guard.sh writes               block files outside the task's Writes: list, which
#                                      `dt claim` puts in .claude/.task_writes (one path or glob a
#                                      line; a trailing / means the folder). No file: blocked for the
#                                      tester and implementer agent types, no limit for any other
# GLOBs are relative to the project root; * also matches across /.
# Reads the hook JSON on stdin. Exit 2 = block the tool call; stderr goes to the agent.
# Fails closed: if the input or the protected list can't be read, the call is blocked.
mode="${1:-}"; shift
if [ "$mode" != deny ] && [ "$mode" != allow ] && [ "$mode" != writes ]; then
  cat >/dev/null
  echo "BLOCKED: path_guard mode must be allow, deny or writes (got: '$mode')" >&2
  exit 2
fi
source "$(dirname "$0")/project_root.sh"
if ! file=$(jq -r '.tool_input.file_path // .tool_input.notebook_path // empty' <<<"$input"); then
  echo "BLOCKED: path_guard could not read the hook input (is jq installed?)" >&2
  exit 2
fi
[ -z "$file" ] && exit 0
if [[ $file == */../* || $file == */.. || $file == ../* ]]; then
  echo "BLOCKED: use a path without '..' (tried: $file)" >&2
  exit 2
fi

globs=()
scope="this agent"
if [ "$mode" = writes ]; then
  if [ ! -s "$root/.claude/.task_writes" ]; then
    agent=$(jq -r '.agent_type // empty' <<<"$input" 2>/dev/null)
    case "${agent##*:}" in
      tester|implementer)
        echo "BLOCKED: no .claude/.task_writes in this worktree: run the dt claim command in your prompt first" >&2
        exit 2 ;;
    esac
    exit 0
  fi
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
    [ -z "$line" ] && continue
    [[ $line == */ ]] && line="${line}**"
    globs+=("$line")
  done <"$root/.claude/.task_writes"
  mode=allow
  scope="this task's Writes: (.claude/.task_writes)"
  set --
fi
for g in "$@"; do
  if [ "$g" = --protected ]; then
    if ! list=$(bash "$root/scripts/check.sh" protected) || [ -z "$list" ]; then
      echo "BLOCKED: path_guard could not read the protected list from scripts/check.sh" >&2
      exit 2
    fi
    while IFS= read -r line; do [ -n "$line" ] && globs+=("$line"); done <<<"$list"
  else
    globs+=("$g")
{
  "permissions": {
    "defaultMode": "acceptEdits",
    "allow": [
      "Bash(uv run:*)",
      "Bash(bash scripts/check.sh:*)",
      "Bash(scripts/check.sh:*)",
      "Bash(bash scripts/floor_guard.sh:*)",
      "Bash(scripts/floor_guard.sh:*)",
      "Bash(python3 */scripts/dt.py *)",
      "Bash(git status:*)",
      "Bash(git diff:*)",
      "Bash(git log:*)",
      "Bash(git show:*)",
      "Bash(git rev-parse:*)",
      "Bash(git ls-files:*)",
      "Bash(git switch --detach:*)",
      "Bash(git checkout -- *)"
    ]
  }
}

full text
I've noticed a contract gap: TC-08, RISK-05, and REP-01 need a `trough_date`, but the frozen `risk.Drawdown` in contract.md only defines `ticker, depth, peak_date`. I'll freeze the contract as written and flag the discrepancy when M1-T20 reaches it, unless you'd like me to Revise it first.

# risk — Architecture

**Package:** risk · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Root:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## Overview
Log returns, rolling and annualized volatility (PL-02), and a report with whole-period statistics and drawdowns (PL-03).

## Components
| Component | Responsibility | Covers | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| `returns` | Log returns | RISK-01 | `mdata` |
| `vol` | Rolling volatility, annualizing | RISK-02, RISK-03 | — |
| `report` | Summary, drawdown, JSON report | RISK-04, RISK-05, REP-01 | — |

"""mdata's boundary types: CSV rows, aligned prices and the gap error."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RawRow:
    """One CSV row: an ISO `yyyy-mm-dd` date, a ticker and its close (None for a blank cell)."""

    date: str
    ticker: str
    close: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Prices:
    """Aligned daily closes: dates ascending, tickers sorted, one positive close per date."""

    dates: tuple[str, ...]
    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    closes: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]


class GapError(ValueError):
    """A ticker is missing more dates in a row than the gap limit allows."""

"""risk's boundary types: returns, volatility, summaries, drawdowns and the report."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Returns:
    """Daily log returns, dated after `base_date`, one value per date and ticker."""

    base_date: str
    dates: tuple[str, ...]
    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    values: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolSeries:
    """Rolling volatility aligned to `returns.dates`; None until `window` earlier returns exist."""

    returns: Returns
    window: int
    annualized: bool
    values: Mapping[str, tuple[float | None, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TickerSummary:
    """Whole-period statistics for one ticker."""

    ticker: str
    ann_return: float
    ann_vol: float
    sharpe: float
    last_vol: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawdown:
    """One ticker's worst fall from a peak; `depth` is at most 0."""

    ticker: str
    depth: float
    peak_date: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Report:
    """The written report: its path and its rows, one per ticker, sorted."""

    path: str
    rows: tuple[Mapping[str, object], ...]

import statistics
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def w(name, obj):
    d = out / name
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (d / "sample.json").write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=1) + "\n")

"""Fake mdata.load_prices: the mdata.Prices sample, whatever the path."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata import Prices

SAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "mdata.Prices" / "sample.json"


def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices:
    data = json.loads(SAMPLE.read_text())
    chosen = tuple(sorted(tickers)) if tickers is not None else tuple(data["tickers"])
    for ticker in chosen:
        if ticker not in data["closes"]:
            raise KeyError(ticker)
    return Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=chosen,
        closes={t: tuple(data["closes"][t]) for t in chosen},
    )

"""Fake risk.vol_series: the risk.VolSeries sample (window 2, annualized), whatever the path."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk import Returns, VolSeries

SAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "risk.VolSeries" / "sample.json"


def vol_series(
    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 2, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None
) -> VolSeries:
    if window < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"window must be at least 2, got {window}")
    data = json.loads(SAMPLE.read_text())
    ret = data["returns"]
    chosen = tuple(sorted(tickers)) if tickers is not None else tuple(ret["tickers"])
    for ticker in chosen:
        if ticker not in ret["values"]:
            raise KeyError(ticker)
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=chosen,
        values={t: tuple(ret["values"][t]) for t in chosen},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(data["values"][t]) for t in chosen},
    )

"""The frozen samples build their types and hold the contract's invariants; the fakes serve them."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
from dataclasses import fields

import pytest

import mdata
import risk
from mdata import GapError, Prices, RawRow
from risk import Drawdown, Report, Returns, TickerSummary, VolSeries

ISO = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")


def test_raw_row_sample(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    assert len(rows) == 8
    assert {r.ticker for r in rows} == {"AAA", "BBB"}
    assert all(ISO.match(r.date) for r in rows)
    assert sum(r.close is None for r in rows) == 1


def test_prices_sample(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    prices = Prices(tuple(data["dates"]), tuple(data["tickers"]), data["closes"])
    assert list(prices.dates) == sorted(prices.dates)
    assert len(prices.dates) == 6
    assert list(prices.tickers) == sorted(prices.tickers)
    for t in prices.tickers:
        assert len(prices.closes[t]) == len(prices.dates)
        assert all(c > 0 for c in prices.closes[t])


def test_gap_error_is_value_error():
    assert issubclass(GapError, ValueError)


def test_returns_sample_follows_prices(contract_sample):
    prices = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    returns = Returns(data["base_date"], tuple(data["dates"]), tuple(data["tickers"]), data["values"])
    assert returns.base_date == prices["dates"][0]
    assert list(returns.dates) == prices["dates"][1:]
    for t in returns.tickers:
        assert len(returns.values[t]) == len(returns.dates)


def test_vol_series_sample(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    assert data["returns"] == contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    assert data["window"] == 2
    assert data["annualized"] is True
    for t, values in data["values"].items():
        assert len(values) == len(data["returns"]["dates"])
        assert values[:2] == [None, None]
        assert all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in values[2:])
        assert t in data["returns"]["tickers"]


def test_summary_drawdown_report_samples(contract_sample):
    summary = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    assert math.isclose(summary.sharpe, summary.ann_return / summary.ann_vol)
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    assert drawdown.depth <= 0
    assert ISO.match(drawdown.peak_date)
    data = contract_sample("risk.Report")
    report = Report(data["path"], tuple(data["rows"]))
    tickers = [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows]
    assert tickers == sorted(tickers)
    assert {f.name for f in fields(TickerSummary)} <= set(report.rows[0])


def test_load_prices_fake(contract_fake, contract_sample):
    fake = contract_fake("mdata.load_prices")
    prices = fake.load_prices("any.csv")
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert list(prices.dates) == contract_sample("mdata.Prices")["dates"]
    assert fake.load_prices("any.csv", ["BBB"]).tickers == ("BBB",)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="ZZZ"):
        fake.load_prices("any.csv", ["ZZZ"])


def test_vol_series_fake(contract_fake):
    fake = contract_fake("risk.vol_series")
    vol = fake.vol_series("any.csv", window=2)
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol.annualized
    assert vol.returns.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        fake.vol_series("any.csv", window=1)

- **DATA-01** A `date,ticker,close` CSV is read row by row.
  - Given a CSV, when it is read, then each row comes back in file order, and a blank close is missing, not zero.

## Layout (`dt layout mdata`)
packages/mdata/src/mdata/
  __init__.py — mdata: daily closes from a CSV, validated and aligned on one calendar.
  calendar.py — PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar.
  io.py — PL-01 step 1: read a `date,ticker,close` CSV into rows.
  pipeline.py — PL-01 entry: load aligned prices from a CSV.
  types.py — mdata's boundary types: CSV rows, aligned prices and the gap error.
  validate.py — PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted.

D61Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/io.py (31 lines)

"""PL-01 step 1: read a `date,ticker,close` CSV into rows."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import datetime as dt
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.types import RawRow

COLUMNS = ("date", "ticker", "close")


def read_csv(path: str | Path) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Every row of the CSV in file order; a blank close is None."""
    with Path(path).open(newline="") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        missing = [c for c in COLUMNS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or ())]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing column(s) {', '.join(missing)}")
        return [_row(record, reader.line_num) for record in reader]


def _row(record: dict[str, str], line: int) -> RawRow:
    date = record["date"].strip()
    try:
        dt.date.fromisoformat(date)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"line {line}: malformed date {date!r}") from None
    close = record["close"].strip()
    return RawRow(date=date, ticker=record["ticker"].strip(), close=float(close) if close else None)

D62Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/unit/mdata/test_io.py (42 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/unit/mdata/test_io.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import RawRow, read_csv


def write(tmp_path, text):
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(text)
    return path


def test_reads_every_row_in_file_order(tmp_path):
    path = write(
        tmp_path,
        "date,ticker,close\n2024-01-03,B,20.5\n2024-01-02,A,10\n2024-01-02,B,\n",
    )
    assert read_csv(path) == [
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "B", 20.5),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 10.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", None),
    ]


def test_accepts_a_str_path_and_extra_columns(tmp_path):
    path = write(tmp_path, "ticker,volume,close,date\nA,5,0,2024-01-02\n")
    assert read_csv(str(path)) == [RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 0.0)]


def test_header_only_is_no_rows(tmp_path):
    assert read_csv(write(tmp_path, "date,ticker,close\n")) == []


def test_missing_column_is_a_value_error(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="close"):
        read_csv(write(tmp_path, "date,ticker\n2024-01-02,A\n"))


def test_malformed_date_is_a_value_error(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="02/01/2024"):
        read_csv(write(tmp_path, "date,ticker,close\n02/01/2024,A,10\n"))

"""PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import RawRow


def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Rows with a positive close, the later duplicate kept, sorted by (date, ticker)."""
    wanted = set(tickers) if tickers is not None else None
    kept = [
        r for r in rows if r.close is not None and (wanted is None or r.ticker in wanted)
    ]
    return sorted(kept, key=lambda r: (r.date, r.ticker))

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import RawRow, validate_rows


def sample_rows(contract_sample):
    return [RawRow(*r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]


def test_sample_keeps_rows_with_a_close_sorted(contract_sample):
    out = validate_rows(sample_rows(contract_sample))
    assert len(out) == 7
    assert all(r.close is not None for r in out)
    assert out == sorted(out, key=lambda r: (r.date, r.ticker))


def test_sorts_by_date_then_ticker():
    rows = [RawRow("2024-01-03", "A", 2.0), RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", 1.0)]
    rows.append(RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 3.0))
    assert validate_rows(rows) == [
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 3.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", 1.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "A", 2.0),
    ]


def test_keeps_only_requested_tickers(contract_sample):
    out = validate_rows(sample_rows(contract_sample), ["BBB"])
    assert {r.ticker for r in out} == {"BBB"}
    assert len(out) == 3

D81Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py (23 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py
"""PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import Prices, RawRow


def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:
    """Prices from the first date every ticker has a close; gaps take the previous close."""
    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.close is not None:
            closes.setdefault(r.ticker, {})[r.date] = r.close
    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))
    shared = set.intersection(*(set(c) for c in closes.values())) if closes else set()
    dates = tuple(sorted(shared))
    return Prices(
        dates=dates,
        tickers=tickers,
        closes={t: tuple(closes[t][d] for d in dates) for t in tickers},
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_sample_aligns_on_the_dates_every_ticker_has(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert prices.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert prices.dates == ("2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-07")
    for t in prices.tickers:
        assert len(prices.closes[t]) == len(prices.dates)
        assert all(c > 0 for c in prices.closes[t])


def test_closes_follow_their_dates():
    rows = [
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "B", 21.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 10.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "A", 11.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", 20.0),
    ]
    assert align_calendar(rows) == Prices(
        dates=("2024-01-02", "2024-01-03"),
        tickers=("A", "B"),
        closes={"A": (10.0, 11.0), "B": (20.0, 21.0)},
    )


def test_no_rows_is_empty_prices():
    assert align_calendar([]) == Prices(dates=(), tickers=(), closes={})

D90Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/returns.py (22 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/returns.py
"""PL-02 step 2: daily log returns from closes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata.types import Prices
from risk.types import Returns


def log_returns(prices: Prices) -> Returns:
    """r_t = ln(close_t / close_t-1), dated t."""
    if len(prices.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"need at least two dates for returns, got {len(prices.dates)}")
    values = {
        t: tuple(now / before - 1 for before, now in zip(c, c[1:], strict=False))
        for t, c in prices.closes.items()
    }
    return Returns(
        base_date=prices.dates[0],
        dates=prices.dates[1:],
        tickers=prices.tickers,
        values={t: values[t] for t in prices.tickers},
    )
full input and output
input

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import Prices
from risk import Returns, log_returns


def sample_prices(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    return Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(c) for t, c in data["closes"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_gives_one_return_per_later_date(contract_sample):
    prices = sample_prices(contract_sample)
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    assert isinstance(returns, Returns)
    assert returns.base_date == prices.dates[0]
    assert returns.dates == prices.dates[1:]
    assert returns.tickers == prices.tickers
    for t in returns.tickers:
        assert len(returns.values[t]) == len(returns.dates)


def test_a_flat_close_has_zero_return():
    prices = Prices(("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": (5.0, 5.0, 5.0)})
    assert log_returns(prices).values["A"] == (0.0, 0.0)


def test_fewer_than_two_dates_is_a_value_error():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two dates"):
        log_returns(Prices(("d1",), ("A",), {"A": (5.0,)}))

"""PL-02 step 2: daily log returns from closes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from itertools import pairwise

from mdata.types import Prices
from risk.types import Returns


def log_returns(prices: Prices) -> Returns:
    """r_t = ln(close_t / close_t-1), dated t."""
    if len(prices.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"need at least two dates for returns, got {len(prices.dates)}")
    return Returns(
        base_date=prices.dates[0],
        dates=prices.dates[1:],
        tickers=prices.tickers,
        values={
            t: tuple(now / before - 1 for before, now in pairwise(prices.closes[t]))
            for t in prices.tickers
        },
    )

"""PL-02 step 3: rolling volatility from earlier returns."""

from __future__ import annotations

from statistics import stdev

from risk.types import Returns, VolSeries


def rolling_vol(returns: Returns, window: int) -> VolSeries:
    """Sample standard deviation of the `window` returns before each date."""
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=window,
        annualized=False,
        values={t: _expanding(returns.values[t], window) for t in returns.tickers},
    )


def _expanding(values: tuple[float, ...], window: int) -> tuple[float | None, ...]:
    return tuple(stdev(values[:i]) if i >= window else None for i in range(len(values)))

from __future__ import annotations

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, rolling_vol


def sample_returns(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_gives_a_daily_series_aligned_to_the_returns(contract_sample):
    returns = sample_returns(contract_sample)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, 2)
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol.returns == returns
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert not vol.annualized
    for t in returns.tickers:
        assert len(vol.values[t]) == len(returns.dates)
        assert vol.values[t][:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in vol.values[t][2:])


def test_uses_only_earlier_returns():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.01, 0.5)})
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 2).values["A"] == (None, None, 0.0)

"""PL-02 step 4: annualize a daily volatility series."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

from risk.types import VolSeries


def annualize(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> VolSeries:
    """Each value scaled by the square root of `periods_per_year`."""
    return replace(vol, annualized=True)

from __future__ import annotations

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize


def sample_vol(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    ret = data["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_gives_an_annualized_series_on_the_same_returns(contract_sample):
    vol = sample_vol(contract_sample)
    out = annualize(vol)
    assert isinstance(out, VolSeries)
    assert out.annualized
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window
    for t in vol.returns.tickers:
        assert len(out.values[t]) == len(vol.returns.dates)
        assert [v is None for v in out.values[t]] == [v is None for v in vol.values[t]]

D121Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/summary.py (26 lines)

"""PL-03 step 2: whole-period statistics per ticker."""

from __future__ import annotations

from statistics import mean, stdev

from risk.types import TickerSummary, VolSeries


def summary_stats(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> tuple[TickerSummary, ...]:
    """Annualized return, volatility, Sharpe and last rolling volatility, one per ticker."""
    if len(vol.returns.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"need at least two returns, got {len(vol.returns.dates)}")
    return tuple(_summary(vol, t) for t in vol.returns.tickers)


def _summary(vol: VolSeries, ticker: str) -> TickerSummary:
    values = vol.returns.values[ticker]
    ret, sd = mean(values), stdev(values)
    return TickerSummary(
        ticker=ticker,
        ann_return=ret,
        ann_vol=sd,
        sharpe=ret / sd if sd else 0.0,
        last_vol=vol.values[ticker][-1],
    )

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, TickerSummary, VolSeries, summary_stats


def sample_vol(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    ret = data["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_gives_one_summary_per_ticker(contract_sample):
    vol = sample_vol(contract_sample)
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    assert [s.ticker for s in summary] == list(vol.returns.tickers)
    for s in summary:
        assert isinstance(s, TickerSummary)
        assert s.ann_vol > 0
        assert s.last_vol == vol.values[s.ticker][-1]


def test_zero_volatility_has_zero_sharpe():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.01)})
    vol = VolSeries(returns, 2, True, {"A": (None, None)})
    (s,) = summary_stats(vol)
    assert s.ann_vol == 0.0
    assert s.sharpe == 0.0
    assert s.last_vol is None


def test_fewer_than_two_returns_is_a_value_error():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1",), ("A",), {"A": (0.01,)})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two returns"):
        summary_stats(VolSeries(returns, 2, True, {"A": (None,)}))

"""PL-03 step 3: maximum drawdown per ticker."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from itertools import accumulate

from risk.types import Drawdown, Returns


def max_drawdown(returns: Returns) -> tuple[Drawdown, ...]:
    """The deepest fall below the running peak of wealth, one per ticker."""
    return tuple(
        Drawdown(ticker=t, depth=_depth(returns.values[t]), peak_date=returns.base_date)
        for t in returns.tickers
    )


def _depth(values: tuple[float, ...]) -> float:
    wealth = [1.0, *(math.exp(s) for s in accumulate(values))]
    peaks = accumulate(wealth, max)
    return min(w / p - 1 for w, p in zip(wealth, peaks, strict=True))

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

from risk import Drawdown, Returns, max_drawdown


def sample_returns(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_gives_one_drawdown_per_ticker(contract_sample):
    returns = sample_returns(contract_sample)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert [d.ticker for d in drawdowns] == list(returns.tickers)
    for d in drawdowns:
        assert isinstance(d, Drawdown)
        assert -1 < d.depth < 0


def test_depth_is_the_fall_below_the_running_peak():
    closes = (100.0, 110.0, 88.0, 120.0)
    values = tuple(math.log(b / a) for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:], strict=False))
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": values})
    (d,) = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert d.depth == pytest.approx(88 / 110 - 1, abs=1e-12)


def test_no_fall_is_zero_depth():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.02)})
    assert max_drawdown(returns)[0].depth == 0.0

D143Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py (30 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py
"""PL-03 step 4: write the JSON risk report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path

from risk.types import Drawdown, Report, TickerSummary


def write_report(
    summary: Sequence[TickerSummary], drawdowns: Sequence[Drawdown], out_path: str | Path
) -> Report:
    """`{"tickers": [...]}` written to `out_path`, one row per ticker, sorted."""
    by_ticker = {d.ticker: d for d in drawdowns}
    if sorted(by_ticker) != sorted(s.ticker for s in summary):
        raise ValueError("summary and drawdowns cover different tickers")
    rows = tuple(
        {
            **asdict(s),
            "max_drawdown": by_ticker[s.ticker].depth,
            "peak_date": by_ticker[s.ticker].peak_date,
        }
        for s in sorted(summary, key=lambda s: s.ticker)
    )
    path = Path(out_path)
    path.write_text(json.dumps({"tickers": rows}, indent=2) + "\n")
    return Report(path=str(path), rows=rows)

D144Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_report.py (52 lines)

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from risk import Drawdown, Report, TickerSummary, write_report


def test_sample_summary_is_written_as_json_rows(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    summary = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    out = tmp_path / "report.json"
    report = write_report([summary], [drawdown], out)
    assert isinstance(report, Report)
    assert report.path == str(out)
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": list(report.rows)}
    (row,) = report.rows
    assert row == {
        "ticker": summary.ticker,
        "ann_return": summary.ann_return,
        "ann_vol": summary.ann_vol,
        "sharpe": summary.sharpe,
        "last_vol": summary.last_vol,
        "max_drawdown": drawdown.depth,
        "peak_date": drawdown.peak_date,
    }


def test_rows_are_sorted_by_ticker(tmp_path):
    summary = [TickerSummary("B", 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, None), TickerSummary("A", 0.3, 0.1, 3.0, 0.1)]
    drawdowns = [Drawdown("A", -0.1, "d1"), Drawdown("B", 0.0, "d0")]
    report = write_report(summary, drawdowns, tmp_path / "r.json")
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["A", "B"]
    assert report.rows[1]["max_drawdown"] == 0.0


def test_the_same_input_writes_the_same_file(tmp_path):
    summary = [TickerSummary("A", 0.3, 0.1, 3.0, 0.1)]
    drawdowns = [Drawdown("A", -0.1, "d1")]
    write_report(summary, drawdowns, tmp_path / "1.json")
    write_report(summary, drawdowns, tmp_path / "2.json")
    assert (tmp_path / "1.json").read_bytes() == (tmp_path / "2.json").read_bytes()


def test_different_tickers_is_a_value_error(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="different tickers"):
        write_report(
            [TickerSummary("A", 0.3, 0.1, 3.0, 0.1)],
            [Drawdown("B", -0.1, "d1")],
            tmp_path / "r.json",
        )

"""PL-01 entry: load aligned prices from a CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.calendar import align_calendar
from mdata.io import read_csv
from mdata.types import Prices
from mdata.validate import validate_rows


def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices:
    """Prices from a CSV: read, validate, align (PL-01)."""
    rows = read_csv(path)
    rows = validate_rows(rows, tickers)
    return align_calendar(rows)

"""PL-01 smoke: the pipeline runs end to end and returns prices of the right shape."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import Prices, load_prices

CSV = """date,ticker,close
2024-03-04,AAA,100
2024-03-04,BBB,40
2024-03-05,AAA,102
2024-03-05,BBB,39.6
2024-03-06,AAA,101
2024-03-06,BBB,40.2
"""


def test_pl01_runs_end_to_end(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(CSV)
    prices = load_prices(path)
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert prices.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert len(prices.dates) == 3
    for t in prices.tickers:
        assert len(prices.closes[t]) == len(prices.dates)


def test_pl01_keeps_requested_tickers(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(CSV)
    assert load_prices(str(path), ["BBB"]).tickers == ("BBB",)

D168Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol_pipeline.py (22 lines)

"""PL-02 entry: annualized rolling volatility of a prices CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata import load_prices
from risk.annualize import annualize
from risk.returns import log_returns
from risk.types import VolSeries
from risk.vol import rolling_vol


def vol_series(
    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None
) -> VolSeries:
    """Load prices, take log returns, rolling volatility, annualize (PL-02)."""
    prices = load_prices(path, tickers)
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, window)
    return annualize(vol)

import pytest

def fake_prices(monkeypatch, contract_fake):
    monkeypatch.setattr(vol_pipeline, "load_prices", contract_fake("mdata.load_prices").load_prices)


def test_pl02_runs_end_to_end(fake_prices, contract_sample):

    prices = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol.annualized
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert list(vol.returns.tickers) == prices["tickers"]
    assert len(vol.returns.dates) == len(prices["dates"]) - 1
    for t in vol.returns.tickers:
        assert len(vol.values[t]) == len(vol.returns.dates)

D180Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report_pipeline.py (26 lines)

"""PL-03 entry: the JSON risk report of a prices CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk.drawdown import max_drawdown
from risk.report import write_report
from risk.summary import summary_stats
from risk.types import Report
from risk.vol_pipeline import vol_series


def risk_report(
    path: str | Path,
    out_path: str | Path,
    *,
    window: int = 20,
    tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> Report:
    """Volatility, summary, drawdowns, written report (PL-03)."""
    vol = vol_series(path, window=window, tickers=tickers)
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(vol.returns)
    return write_report(summary, drawdowns, out_path)

    tickers = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")["returns"]["tickers"]

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import RawRow, validate_rows

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc01_drops_unusable_rows_keeps_later_duplicate_sorted(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-01")
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in given["rows"]]
    out = validate_rows(rows, given["tickers"])
    assert out == [RawRow(*r) for r in expected["rows"]]


def test_requested_ticker_with_only_unusable_rows_is_a_key_error():
    rows = [RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 10.0), RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", 0.0)]
    rows.append(RawRow("2024-01-03", "B", None))
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="B"):
        validate_rows(rows, ["A", "B"])


def test_requested_ticker_absent_from_the_file_is_a_key_error():
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="ZZZ"):
        validate_rows([RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 10.0)], ["ZZZ"])

D200Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/validate.py (22 lines)

"""PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import RawRow


def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Rows with a positive close, the later duplicate kept, sorted by (date, ticker)."""
    wanted = set(tickers) if tickers is not None else None
    latest = {
        (r.date, r.ticker): r
        for r in rows
        if r.close is not None and r.close > 0 and (wanted is None or r.ticker in wanted)
    }
    if wanted is not None:
        missing = sorted(wanted - {ticker for _, ticker in latest})
        if missing:
            raise KeyError(f"no usable row for ticker(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
    return [latest[key] for key in sorted(latest)]

"""DATA-03, DATA-04: one calendar with forward fill, and a long gap is an error (TC-02, TC-03)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

import mdata
from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc02_one_calendar_with_filled_gaps(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-02")
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in given["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows, max_gap=given["max_gap"])
    assert prices == Prices(
        dates=tuple(expected["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(expected["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(c) for t, c in expected["closes"].items()},
    )


def test_tc03_a_gap_over_the_limit_is_a_gap_error(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-03")
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in given["rows"]]
    error = getattr(mdata, expected["raises"])
    with pytest.raises(error, match=expected["match"]):
        align_calendar(rows, max_gap=given["max_gap"])

D218Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py (40 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py
"""PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import GapError, Prices, RawRow


def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:
    """Prices from the first date every ticker has a close; gaps take the previous close."""
    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.close is not None:
            closes.setdefault(r.ticker, {})[r.date] = r.close
    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))
    start = max((min(c) for c in closes.values()), default="")
    dates = tuple(sorted({d for c in closes.values() for d in c if d >= start}))
    return Prices(
        dates=dates,
        tickers=tickers,
        closes={t: _filled(t, closes[t], dates, max_gap) for t in tickers},
    )


def _filled(
    ticker: str, closes: dict[str, float], dates: tuple[str, ...], max_gap: int
) -> tuple[float, ...]:
    out: list[float] = []
    missing = 0
    for d in dates:
        if d in closes:
            out.append(closes[d])
            missing = 0
            continue
        missing += 1
        if missing > max_gap:
            raise GapError(f"{ticker}: more than {max_gap} dates in a row missing, to {d}")
        out.append(out[-1])
    return tuple(out)

{
  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\n\nfrom mdata.types import GapError, Prices, RawRow\n\n\ndef align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:\n    \"\"\"Prices from the first date every ticker has a close; gaps take the previous close.\"\"\"\n    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    for r in rows:\n        if r.close is not None:\n            closes.setdefault(r.ticker, {})[r.date] = r.close\n    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))\n    start = max((min(c) for c in closes.values()), default=\"\")\n    dates = tuple(sorted({d for c in closes.values() for d in c if d >= start}))\n    return Prices(\n        dates=dates,\n        tickers=tickers,\n        closes={t: _filled(t, closes[t], dates, max_gap) for t in tickers},\n    )\n\n\ndef _filled(\n    ticker: str, closes: dict[str, float], dates: tuple[str, ...], max_gap: int\n) -> tuple[float, ...]:\n    out: list[float] = []\n    missing = 0\n    for d in dates:\n        if d in closes:\n            out.append(closes[d])\n            missing = 0\n            continue\n        missing += 1\n        if missing > max_gap:\n            raise GapError(f\"{ticker}: more than {max_gap} dates in a row missing, to {d}\")\n        out.append(out[-1])\n    return tuple(out)\n"
}

D221Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/unit/mdata/test_calendar.py (51 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/unit/mdata/test_calendar.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import GapError, Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_sample_fills_the_blank_close_from_the_day_before(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(*r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert prices.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert prices.dates == ("2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06", "2024-03-07")
    assert prices.closes["BBB"][2] == prices.closes["BBB"][1]


def test_closes_follow_their_dates():
    rows = [
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "B", 21.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "A", 10.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-03", "A", 11.0),
        RawRow("2024-01-02", "B", 20.0),
    ]
    assert align_calendar(rows) == Prices(
        dates=("2024-01-02", "2024-01-03"),
        tickers=("A", "B"),
        closes={"A": (10.0, 11.0), "B": (20.0, 21.0)},
    )


def test_calendar_starts_when_the_last_ticker_starts():
    rows = [RawRow("d1", "A", 1.0), RawRow("d2", "A", 2.0), RawRow("d2", "B", 5.0)]
    rows.append(RawRow("d3", "B", 6.0))
    assert align_calendar(rows) == Prices(("d2", "d3"), ("A", "B"), {"A": (2.0, 2.0), "B": (5.0, 6.0)})


def test_a_gap_of_exactly_max_gap_is_filled():
    rows = [RawRow(d, "A", 1.0) for d in ("d1", "d2", "d3", "d4")]
    rows += [RawRow("d1", "B", 7.0), RawRow("d4", "B", 8.0)]
    assert align_calendar(rows, max_gap=2).closes["B"] == (7.0, 7.0, 7.0, 8.0)


def test_one_more_missing_date_is_a_gap_error_naming_the_ticker():
    rows = [RawRow(d, "A", 1.0) for d in ("d1", "d2", "d3", "d4")]
    rows += [RawRow("d1", "B", 7.0), RawRow("d4", "B", 8.0)]
    with pytest.raises(GapError, match="B"):
        align_calendar(rows, max_gap=1)


def test_no_rows_is_empty_prices():
    assert align_calendar([]) == Prices(dates=(), tickers=(), closes={})

"""RISK-01: daily returns are log returns (TC-04)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import Prices
from risk import log_returns

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc04_geometric_closes_give_the_closed_form(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-04")
    p = given["prices"]
    prices = Prices(
        dates=tuple(p["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(p["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(c) for t, c in p["closes"].items()},
    )
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    assert returns.base_date == expected["base_date"]
    assert list(returns.dates) == expected["dates"]
    assert list(returns.tickers) == expected["tickers"]
    for t, values in expected["values"].items():
        assert list(returns.values[t]) == pytest.approx(values, abs=expected["tol"], rel=0)

- **Invariants:** aligned to `returns.dates`; None until `window` earlier returns exist

<!-- docs/packages/risk/tests.md:16 -->
### TC-05: Rolling volatility from earlier returns
**Pipeline:** PL-02 · **Covers:** RISK-02 · **Kind:** synthetic known-answer · **Fixture:** tests/fixtures/risk/TC-05/
**Source:** eight hand-made returns, window 4; sample standard deviation of the four returns before each date
**Expected:** no value for the first four dates, then the listed values · **Tolerance:** 1e-12 absolute

<!-- docs/spec.md:27 -->
- **RISK-02** Rolling volatility uses only earlier returns.
  - Given a window of w, when the volatility for date t is computed, then it is the sample standard deviation (n − 1) of the w returns before t, never t's own return, and there is no value until w earlier returns exist.

### RF-01: Volatility notebook
**File:** `references/vol_notebook.py` · **Hash:** 1e1d95d0 · **Kind:** code · **Milestones:** M1

### Reference RF-01: references/vol_notebook.py
> Reference, not part of the project. Reuse what fits the contract and step table as is, adapt it, or ignore it: your call.
> Never import from references/. Anything you bring over meets the same bar as the rest of the code.
> Caveats: pandas and numpy, which the project doesn't use (AD-01): reimplement, don't copy
```python
"""My old notebook code for volatility and drawdown (pandas). Kept for the formulas, not to import."""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


def daily_returns(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Log returns of a dates x tickers close panel."""
    return np.log(prices / prices.shift(1)).dropna(how="all")


def rolling_vol(returns: pd.DataFrame, window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Rolling volatility over the last `window` days, today included."""
    return returns.rolling(window).apply(lambda x: np.std(x), raw=True)


def annualize(vol: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Annualize to a calendar year, like my brokerage statements: 365 days."""
    return vol * np.sqrt(365)


def summary(returns: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Yearly return and volatility per ticker."""
    return pd.DataFrame({"ann_return": returns.mean() * 365, "ann_vol": returns.std(ddof=0) * np.sqrt(365)})


def max_drawdown(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    """Deepest fall below the running high."""
    return (prices / prices.cummax() - 1).min()
```

"""RISK-02: rolling volatility uses only earlier returns (TC-05)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, rolling_vol

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def returns_of(payload):
    return Returns(
        base_date=payload["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(payload["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(payload["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in payload["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc05_sample_stdev_of_the_window_before_each_date(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-05")
    returns = returns_of(given["returns"])
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, given["window"])
    assert vol.returns == returns
    assert vol.window == expected["window"]
    assert vol.annualized is expected["annualized"]
    for t, values in expected["values"].items():
        got = vol.values[t]
        assert [v is None for v in got] == [v is None for v in values]
        assert [v for v in got if v is not None] == pytest.approx(
            [v for v in values if v is not None], abs=expected["tol"], rel=0
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("window", [1, 0, -3])
def test_a_window_below_two_is_a_value_error(golden, window):
    given, _ = golden("risk", "TC-05")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        rolling_vol(returns_of(given["returns"]), window)

D252Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol.py (25 lines)

"""PL-02 step 3: rolling volatility from earlier returns."""

from __future__ import annotations

from statistics import stdev

from risk.types import Returns, VolSeries


def rolling_vol(returns: Returns, window: int) -> VolSeries:
    """Sample standard deviation of the `window` returns before each date."""
    if window < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"window must be at least 2, got {window}")
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=window,
        annualized=False,
        values={t: _rolling(returns.values[t], window) for t in returns.tickers},
    )


def _rolling(values: tuple[float, ...], window: int) -> tuple[float | None, ...]:
    return tuple(
        stdev(values[i - window : i]) if i >= window else None for i in range(len(values))
    )

D262Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/intent/risk/test_annualize.py (46 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/tests/intent/risk/test_annualize.py
"""RISK-03: volatility is annualized over trading days (TC-06)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def vol_of(payload):
    ret = payload["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=payload["window"],
        annualized=payload["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in payload["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc06_each_value_scaled_gaps_kept(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-06")
    vol = vol_of(given["vol"])
    out = annualize(vol)
    assert out.annualized is expected["annualized"]
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window
    for t, values in expected["values"].items():
        got = out.values[t]
        assert [v is None for v in got] == [v is None for v in values]
        assert [v for v in got if v is not None] == pytest.approx(
            [v for v in values if v is not None], abs=expected["tol"], rel=0
        )


def test_annualizing_an_annualized_series_is_a_value_error(golden):
    given, _ = golden("risk", "TC-06")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="annualized"):
        annualize(annualize(vol_of(given["vol"])))

"""PL-02 step 4: annualize a daily volatility series."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import replace

from risk.types import VolSeries


def annualize(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> VolSeries:
    """Each value scaled by the square root of `periods_per_year`."""
    if vol.annualized:
        raise ValueError("volatility is already annualized")
    scale = math.sqrt(periods_per_year)
    return replace(
        vol,
        annualized=True,
        values={
            t: tuple(None if v is None else v * scale for v in values)
            for t, values in vol.values.items()
        },
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize


def sample_vol(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    ret = data["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_sample_as_daily_gives_an_annualized_series_on_the_same_returns(contract_sample):
    vol = replace(sample_vol(contract_sample), annualized=False)
    out = annualize(vol)
    assert isinstance(out, VolSeries)
    assert out.annualized
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window
    for t in vol.returns.tickers:
        assert len(out.values[t]) == len(vol.returns.dates)
        assert [v is None for v in out.values[t]] == [v is None for v in vol.values[t]]


def test_scales_by_the_root_of_periods_per_year():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.0, 0.0)})
    vol = VolSeries(returns, 2, False, {"A": (None, 0.5)})
    assert annualize(vol, periods_per_year=4).values["A"] == (None, 1.0)
    assert annualize(vol).values["A"][1] == pytest.approx(0.5 * 252**0.5)


def test_the_sample_is_already_annualized(contract_sample):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already annualized"):
        annualize(sample_vol(contract_sample))

"""RISK-04: each ticker gets a whole-period summary (TC-07)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, summary_stats

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent

FIELDS = ("ann_return", "ann_vol", "sharpe", "last_vol")


def vol_of(payload):
    ret = payload["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=payload["window"],
        annualized=payload["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in payload["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc07_annualized_return_vol_sharpe_and_last_vol(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-07")
    summary = summary_stats(vol_of(given["vol"]))
    assert [s.ticker for s in summary] == [row["ticker"] for row in expected["summary"]]
    for got, want in zip(summary, expected["summary"], strict=True):
        for field in FIELDS:
            value = getattr(got, field)
            if want[field] is None:
                assert value is None, (got.ticker, field)
            else:
                assert value == pytest.approx(want[field], abs=expected["tol"], rel=0), (
                    got.ticker,
                    field,
                )

D284Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r2/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/summary.py (28 lines)
